In case of intentional harm to the environment, to other activities conducted in the vicinity, to the well-being of neighboring populations or to the health of animals located in the vicinity, all costs arising from measures taken for the restoration of the premises are borne by the operator.

In all cases, the judge may order the suspension of the aquaculture operator license for a period of six months to twelve months or permanent withdrawal.

Article 510-18

Is punished by imprisonment of five years and a fine of 500,000 francs, anyone who refuses access to surveillance agents to a commercial or scientific aquaculture establishment.
Refusal of access is punished by imprisonment of three months and a fine of 500,000 francs in the case of a subsistence aquaculture establishment.

Article 510-19

Is punished by imprisonment of five years and a fine of 500,000 francs, anyone who refuses to allow a surveillance agent to take samples of water, soil, products used, aquaculture products and other elements related to aquaculture activities in the case of a commercial or scientific aquaculture establishment.

In the case of subsistence aquaculture, the imprisonment penalty is three months and a fine of 500,000 francs.

Section 3: Aquaculture establishments and species

Article 510-20

Is punished by imprisonment of one month and a fine of 500,000 francs without prejudice to damages, anyone who, in violation of the provisions of this chapter, installs and operates an aquaculture establishment.

TITRE XI: OFFENCES RELATING TO PASTORAL ACTIVITIES

[Law No. 01-004/of 27 February 2001 on the Pastoral Charter of Mali]

Chapitre unique: Offences and penalties

Article 511-1

Is punished by a fine of 18,000 francs, without prejudice to damage caused to third parties, anyone who:

1°) occupies or obstructs a pastoral route or a stopover site or encroaches on their right of way;

2°) exploits water resources contrary to accepted rules or pollutes them;

3°) moves animals outside pastoral routes;

4°) contravenes the provisions relating to the opening periods of harvested fields for grazing animals.

Article 511-2

Is punished by imprisonment of six months and a fine of 100,000 francs, without prejudice to damage caused to third parties, anyone who:

1°) damages the property of others by allowing animals to roam freely;

2°) without an environmental impact study, implements a programme or project likely to result in the suppression or disappearance of pastoral resources, in whole or in part;

3°) contravenes a transhumance calendar.

TITRE XII: OFFENCES IN MINING MATTERS

[Law No. 2023-040/of 29 August 2023 on the Mining Code in the Republic of Mali]

Chapitre unique: Offences and sanctions

Without prejudice to the provisions relating to the withdrawal of mining titles and authorisations and civil actions for compensation for damage caused, the following sanctions are applicable:

Article 512-1

Are punished by imprisonment of two years and a fine of 5,000,000 francs:

1°) those who make a false declaration to obtain a mining title;

2°) those who oppose in any manner whatsoever the occupation of a mining perimeter by the holder of the mining title;

3°) those who carry out the analysis of samples outside Mali without prior authorisation from the administration in charge of mines;

4°) those who agree to have children work on their site.

Article 512-2

Are punished by imprisonment of three years and a fine of 5,000,000 francs:

1°) those who engage in research or exploitation work on mineral substances subject to the mining regime without holding an appropriate title;

2°) those who engage in illegal gold panning;

3°) those who provide aid and assistance to unauthorised prospectors and operators;

4°) those who do not declare, at the end of the mining title, the definitive cessation of work relating to site rehabilitation provided for by the regulations in force;

5°) those who hold mining titles and who do not comply within the prescribed time limit with the instructions of the administration in charge of Mines relating to measures for the protection of the safety and health of personnel, public safety and health, soil conservation, flora, fauna, communication routes, the solidity of public or private buildings, the flow or quality of water of any nature;

6°) those who hold mining titles and quarry exploitation authorisations who falsify their extraction, sale and/or shipment registers.

Article 512-3

Holders of large-scale or small-scale mining exploitation permits and holders of industrial quarry exploitation authorisations who do not keep their extraction, sale or shipment registers regularly, or who refuse to produce them to qualified officials and agents of the administration in charge of Mines, may, after a three-month formal notice by the minister in charge of Mines or the administration in charge of Mines that has remained unsuccessful, be deprived of their titles without prejudice to the payment of a fine of 5,000,000 francs.

Article 512-4

Is punished by imprisonment of three months and a fine of 2,000,000 francs:

1°) any person other than holders of mining titles and holders of artisanal quarry opening authorisations or industrial quarry exploitation authorisations, executing a borehole, an underground work, an excavation work, whatever the purpose, with the exception of wells for domestic use, the depth of which exceeds ten metres, who does not make the declaration either to the administration in charge of Mines, or to the Territorial Collectivity of the place where the work is carried out, which informs the administration in charge of Mines;

2°) any person who does not make the prior declaration to the administration in charge of Mines before any geophysical survey, any geochemical prospecting campaign or heavy mineral studies.

Article 512-5

Are punished by imprisonment of two years and a fine of 5,000,000 francs:

1°) those who, without a mining title and in an illicit manner, engage in the transport, processing and marketing of mineral substances extracted in Mali;

2°) those who exploit mineral substances subject to the quarry regime not covered by a quarry opening or exploitation authorisation;

3°) those who engage in the exploitation of mineral substances by dredging.

Article 512-6

Officials and sworn agents of the administration in charge of Mines may requisition agents of the public force.

Violence and assault exercised against officials and agents of the administration in charge of Mines in the exercise of their functions are punished by the penalties provided for by the provisions of this Code.

It is prohibited for any official or agent of the State in service in the public administration:

1°) to take directly or indirectly an interest in a research or exploitation enterprise of mineral substances over the entire extent of the territory;

2°) to exercise directly or through an intermediary service provisions with holders of research or exploitation mining titles;

3°) to disclose or give confidential information made available to him in the context of his work or even after cessation of service whatever the reason for a period of at least four years.

Any violation of these provisions results in the dismissal of the agent and the loss of civil servant status without prejudice to sanctions provided for by the competent courts.

Article 512-7

Are punished by imprisonment of two years and a fine of 5,000,000 francs, those who violate the provisions of mining regulations concerning public safety and health and the preservation of the environment, and in particular:

1°) those who conduct works without complying with the regulations in force;

2°) those who oppose the implementation of prescribed measures relating to site rehabilitation provided for by the implementing decree;

3°) those who proceed with the discharge of waste rock or mud into quarries or pits in which mineral resources exist without prior authorization from the minister in charge of Mines.

Without prejudice to the sanctions provided for by this article, the State reserves the right to require complete reparation of damage or harm caused to the environment by all means at its disposal. The offender is entirely responsible for the damage and harm caused to the environment until complete reparation thereof.

Article 512-8

Are punished by a fine of 1,000,000 francs, holders of mining titles who do not submit their quarterly report within fifteen days.

Are punished by a fine of 5,000,000 francs, holders of mining titles and holders of industrial quarry operating authorization who do not submit their annual report within forty-five days.

Article 512- 9

The penalties and fines provided for by the provisions of this title are doubled in case of recidivism within five years following the expiration or prescription of the penalty.

Without prejudice to the sanctions provided for by the provisions of this title, mineral substances illicitly extracted as well as the means, objects, machines, equipment and instruments that contributed to the offenses cited above are seized, confiscated for the benefit of the State without possibility of settlement. All costs incurred are borne by the offender.

TITRE XIII: OFFENSES RELATING TO LOCAL CONTENT IN THE MINING SECTOR

[Law No. 2023-041 of August 29, 2023 relating to Local Content in the mining sector]

Chapitre unique: Offenses and penalties

Article 513-1

Failure to comply with obligations related to Local Content exposes in particular to the following sanctions:

1°) termination of the contract under the conditions set by the regulations;

2°) payment of the fine provided for in Article 513-2 below;

3°) for subcontractors, suppliers and service providers, exclusion from the competitive bidding platform and prohibition from concluding contracts related to oil and gas activities.

Article 513-2

Natural persons who violate obligations related to Local Content:

1°) are punished by imprisonment of ten days and a fine corresponding to the amount of the share of services or supply of goods not performed without formal notice;

2°) are punished by imprisonment of three months and a fine of 75,000,000 francs for failure to transmit the goods and services procurement plan or for failure to transmit the execution report within the required time limits, after a formal notice of seven clear days that remained without response. The fine is increased by twenty-five percent per day of delay;

3°) are punished by imprisonment of two years and a fine of 200,000,000 francs in case of recidivism, the fine is increased by twenty-five percent per day of delay. In case of persistence, withdrawal of the title is pronounced in accordance with the provisions of the Mining Code.

The same provisions are applicable to legal persons excluding imprisonment penalties.

Article 513-3

Violations of the provisions of the law relating to local content are recorded by the Permanent Secretariat of Local Content "SPCL".

Fines are collected by the Public Treasury. The terms of their distribution are specified by joint order of the ministers in charge of Mines and Finance.

LIVRE VI

POLICE CONTRAVENTIONS

Chapitre 1: Classification of contraventions

Article 601-1

Contraventions are divided according to their severity into three classes and the following penalties are applicable to them:	first class contraventions: a fine not exceeding 50,000 francs;	second class contraventions: a fine exceeding 50,000 francs and not exceeding 75,000 francs;	third class contraventions: a fine exceeding 75,000 francs and not exceeding 100,000 francs.

Article 601-2

Confiscation may be applied as an additional penalty when an express provision indicates it.
Police penalties depriving or restricting rights may be applied to legal persons in the cases specified by this Code.

Chapitre 2: First class contraventions

Section1

First class contraventions punishable by a fine of 50,000 francs

Article 602-1: Are punished by first class contravention penalties

1°) Violations of regulations:	those who violate regulations legally made and published by the administrative or municipal authority;
2°) Refusal to clean streets:	those who neglect to clean streets or passages in sectors where this responsibility is left to the charge of residents;
3°) Failure to observe road regulations:	those who neglect or refuse to execute regulations or orders concerning minor roads or disobey the summons from the administrative or municipal authority to repair or demolish constructions threatening to collapse.
4°) Obstruction of public roads:	those who, without permission from the competent authority, obstruct the public road either by depositing or allowing to be deposited materials or any objects that prevent or diminish the freedom or safety of passage, or by digging excavations therein; those who, in the case where the deposit is permitted, have not removed the deposited objects within the time limit set by the authority, or who neglect to light the materials or objects they have deposited on the public road or excavations they have dug therein.

5°) Negligence or imprudence in securing streets, roads and fields:
those who leave in streets, roads, fields and all public places tools, machines, instruments or weapons that can be used by thieves and other wrongdoers; the judgment orders in this case the confiscation of the aforementioned weapons, instruments or machines;

6°) Gleaning in fields:
those who glean in fields not yet entirely cleared of their crops;

7°) negligence in the maintenance of ovens, chimneys or factories:
those who neglect to maintain, repair or clean ovens, chimneys or factories where fire is used;

8°) Public drunkenness:
those who are found in a state of manifest intoxication likely to disturb public order in streets, roads, squares, cafés, cabarets or other public places.

9°) Forced entry or entry in a state of intoxication into stadiums or performance halls:
those who enter a sports enclosure or a performance hall by force or in a state of intoxication.

10°) Failure to hold the secure biometric national identity card:
any Malian citizen aged at least eighteen years, not holding the secure biometric national identity card in current validity.

Section 2

First class contraventions punishable by a fine of less than 50,000 francs:

Paragraph 1: Offences relating to road traffic policing

[Decree No. 2023-0509 PT-RM of 12 September 2023 establishing the conditions for the use of roads open to public traffic and the putting into circulation of vehicles]

Article 602-2

Any person who contravenes the obligation to immobilize the vehicle or the injunctions addressed to them by agents authorized to record offences relating to road traffic policing, in particular for offences related to vehicle equipment or when the driver or their load causes damage to the road or its appurtenances, is punished by a fine of 15,000 francs.

Article 602-3

Any person who offers for sale or sells a vehicle or trailer in violation of the provisions relating to vehicle registration is punished by a fine of 18,000 francs.

Article 602-4

Any driver of a vehicle who knowingly fails to comply with an order to stop issued by an agent responsible for recording offences and wearing the external and visible insignia of their capacity, or who refuses to submit to the prescribed legal checks concerning the vehicle or its driver, is punished by a fine of 18,000 francs.

Article 602-5

Any person who has contravened the provisions concerning the following is punished by a fine of 15,000 francs:

a)	directions imposed on traffic;

b)	crossing and overtaking;

c)	the speed of motor vehicles with or without trailers;

d)	road intersections and right of way;

e)	significant changes of direction;

f)	parking outside built-up areas when visibility is insufficient near a road intersection, at the top of a hill or on a bend, or at night in unlit places;

g)	the use of lighting, signalling and advance signalling devices outside the cases of change of direction provided for in the provisions of the decree establishing the conditions for the use of roads open to public traffic and the putting into circulation of vehicles;

h)	level crossings;

i)	working conditions in road transport;

j)	signals requiring a complete stop;

k)	prohibitions or restrictions on traffic provided for on certain routes for certain categories of vehicles or for vehicles carrying out certain transport;

l)	obligations or prohibitions relating to the use of roads with specialized traffic;

m)	traffic restrictions enacted on the occasion of races and sporting events;

n)	the wearing of protective helmets concerning drivers and passengers of motorcycles, mopeds and motor-assisted cycles;

o)	stopping or parking in any place where they would constitute a danger;

p)	the transport, for commercial purposes or otherwise, of a number of persons greater than that which the vehicle used is authorized to carry on board;

q)	the use of a device equipped with a screen placed in the field of vision of the driver of a vehicle in motion and not constituting a driving or navigation aid.

Any person who commits a new offence against the provisions regulating parking in built-up areas when they have, in the six months preceding this offence, committed in the same built-up area at least two offences of the same nature followed by conviction is punished by a fine of 15,000 francs.

The fine is increased to the maximum when the number of offences previously committed under the same conditions is at least four. This same penalty is incurred from the second conviction in the cases referred to in paragraph 1 of this article.

Article 602-6

Any person who has contravened the provisions concerning the following is punished by a fine of 5,000 francs:

a)	the driving of vehicles and animals, including parking and stopping, outside the cases provided for in the other articles of the decree establishing the conditions for the use of roads open to public traffic and the putting into circulation of vehicles;

b)	the speed of animals and vehicles without motors with or without trailers or semi-trailers;

c)	the use of audible warning devices;

d)	the wearing of seat belts and the transport of children provided for in the provisions relating to the equipment of vehicle users referred to in the decree cited above.

Article 602-7

Any person who has contravened the rules concerning the regulations on rain barriers and the crossing of bridges is punished by a fine of 18,000 francs.

In case of recidivism, the fine is doubled.

Article 602-8

Any person who has contravened the provisions concerning the following is punished by a fine of 18,000 francs:

a)	the organization of races and sporting events;

b)	the crossing of ferries.

When the fault, negligence or imprudence of a user has caused damage to a public road or its appurtenances, said user is punished by a fine of 18,000 francs, without prejudice, where applicable, to the more severe penalties provided for by Article 602-1-4 of this Code. They are, in addition, sentenced to reimbursement of repair costs for the benefit of the State or the Territorial Authority that suffered the damage.

Article 602-9

Any person who, having placed on a road open to public traffic or in its immediate vicinity an object or device likely to cause a disturbance to traffic, does not comply with the injunctions addressed with a view to the removal of said object or device, by one of the agents authorized to record offences relating to road traffic, is punished by a fine of 15,000 francs.

Article 602-10

Any person who contravenes the special provisions of the decree cited above concerning pedestrian traffic is punished by a fine of 5,000 francs.

Article 602-11

Without prejudice to the requirements of the law governing road traffic, any person who contravenes the provisions of the decree cited above concerning the following is punished by a fine of 18,000 francs:

a)	the brakes of motor vehicles other than motorcycles and mopeds;

b)	the number of authorized seats for public passenger transport vehicles;

c)	the speedometer.

In case of new offences committed by the same person, a fine of 18,000 francs is imposed.

Any carrier or vehicle owner who contravenes the provisions adopted by joint order of the minister responsible for Transport, the minister responsible for Security and the minister responsible for Finance concerning the control of axle load of vehicles, is punished according to the case by the following penalties:

Overload of 1 to 5 tonnes ——————5,000 francs per tonne

Overload of 6 to 10 tonnes —————10,000 francs per tonne

Overload of 11 to 15 tonnes —————15,000 francs per tonne Overload of more than 15 tonnes————18,000 francs per tonne

Article 602-12

Any person who contravenes, except those mentioned in the preceding article, the following is punished by a fine of 15,000 francs:

1°) the particular provisions applicable to motor vehicles and vehicle combinations;

2°) the particular provisions applicable to agricultural vehicles and equipment and to certain special machinery;

3°) the particular provisions applicable to motorcycles, mopeds, motor tricycles and quadricycles and their trailers.

However, offences concerning cycles and their equipment expose their perpetrators to a fine of 5,000 francs.

Article 602-13

Without prejudice to subsequent justification of possession of the document or documents not presented, any person who was unable to immediately present during a road check one of the documents listed below is punished by a fine of 3,000 francs:

1.	their driving licence or driving authorisation;
2.	the vehicle registration certificate and, where applicable, that of the trailer or semi-trailer or the provisional receipts;
3.	the insurance certificate provided for by the Treaty establishing an integrated organisation of the insurance industry in African States;
4.	the special technical inspection certificate;
5.	where applicable:	the exceptional transport authorisation;	the public transport authorisation;	the authorisation to operate a taxi or a hire car with driver;	the route sheet relating to a hire car without driver;	the road crossing fee ticket;	the consignment note.
Any person who, invited to justify within a period of ten days possession of the authorisations and documents mentioned in the first paragraph, has not presented these documents before the expiry of that period is punished by a fine of 18,000 francs.

Article 602-14

Without prejudice to the more severe penalties applicable to offences against the rules concerning the driving of vehicles, vehicles and their equipment, any person who is punished by a fine of 18,000 francs:

a)	causes to circulate on roads open to public traffic a motor vehicle or a towed vehicle without registration plates or the external inscriptions required by this Code;

b)	voluntarily puts or keeps in circulation a motor or towed vehicle fitted with plates or inscriptions not corresponding to the type of vehicle or to that of the user;

c)	puts or keeps in circulation a motor or towed vehicle without holding the authorisations or administrative documents required for the circulation of that vehicle or who has not presented the said vehicle for technical inspection within the regulatory time limits;

d)	makes use of authorisations or administrative documents required for the circulation of a motor or towed vehicle which they know to be expired or cancelled;

e)	drives a vehicle without having applied for the extension of their driving licence or driving authorisation, or without having complied with the conditions of validity thereof.

Paragraphe 2

Traffic offences contained in Decree No. 2019-0590/P-RIM of 31 July 2019 establishing the rules applicable to navigation and transport on navigable waterways in the Republic of Mali

Article 602-15

Any person who contravenes the provisions concerning is punished by a fine of 15,000 francs:
the directions imposed on navigation;
the requirements relating to the registration of craft;
compliance with the useful load of the craft;
general documents;
the rules of use relating to the safety of the craft, persons on board and their property;
the use of lighting and signalling devices outside the cases provided for in the decree establishing the rules applicable to navigation and transport on navigable waterways in the Republic of Mali.

Article 602-16

Failure to present one of the authorisations or administrative documents required for the navigation of a boat or motorised craft is punished by a fine of 3,000 francs.

Any person who, invited to justify within a period of ten days possession of the authorisations or administrative documents mentioned above, does not present these documents before the expiry of that period is punished by a fine of 18,000 francs.

Article 602-17

Without prejudice to the provisions of this Code, any person who is punished by a fine of 18,000 francs:

a)	causes to navigate on navigable waterways, apart from small craft, a motorised craft without registration plates;

b)	voluntarily puts or keeps in navigation, apart from small craft, a motorised craft fitted with plates or inscriptions not corresponding to the type of craft or to that of the user;

c)	puts or keeps in navigation, apart from small craft, a motorised craft without holding the authorisations or administrative documents required for the navigation of that boat or who has not presented the said boat for technical inspection within the regulatory time limits;

d)	makes use of authorisations or administrative documents required for the navigation of a motorised craft which they knew to be expired or cancelled;

e)	operates a boat without having applied for the extension of their licence or navigation authorisation, or without having complied with the conditions of validity thereof.

Chapter 3: Second-class misdemeanours

Article 603-1: Are punished by the penalties for second-class misdemeanours

1°) Negligence in signalling stored materials:
Those who neglect to signal by means of lighting or by any other means, materials stored or holes dug by them on the public highway.

2°) Entry onto another person's land:
those who, being neither owners, nor tenants, nor usufructuaries, nor users of a land or of a right of way, or who being neither agents nor employees of these persons enter or pass onto this land or part of this land, prepared, sown or bearing a crop ready to be removed; those who drive or allow livestock, draught animals, pack animals or riding animals to pass onto another person's land that is sown or bearing a crop.

3°) Use of vehicles with tinted windows:
those who drive in vehicles with tinted windows contrary to the regulations enacted by the competent authorities.

4°) Non-public insults:
those who, without having been provoked, utter non-public insults against someone.

5°) Minor violence and throwing of filth at persons:
the perpetrators and accomplices of brawls, assaults or minor violence and those who, inadvertently, have thrown filth at someone.

6°) Wandering of the mentally ill:
those who allow mentally ill persons placed under their control or care to wander knowing that they are likely to attack persons or cause damage to the property of others.

7°) Straying of dangerous animals:
those who allow dangerous animals placed under their supervision or care to stray or wander in such a way that they may attack passers-by or cause harm to others or who incite said animals to attack or do not prevent them from attacking passers-by.

8°) Throwing of harmful things onto the public highway:
those who, voluntarily or recklessly, throw onto the public highway objects likely to injure passers-by by their fall or to soil their clothing.

9°) Voluntary throwing of objects at another person's property:
those who throw stones or other hard objects or filth at the vehicles, houses, buildings and property of others, without prejudice, where applicable, to the penalties provided for by articles 321-10, 435-9 first paragraph and 435-14 of this Code.

10°) Death or injuries caused to animals:
those who, either through clumsiness, imprudence, inattention, negligence, failure to observe regulations, or by throwing stones or other hard objects, cause the death or injury of animals or livestock belonging to others, without prejudice, where applicable, to the penalties provided for by articles 435-12 and 435-14 of this Code.

11°) Ill-treatment inflicted on animals:
those who exercise without necessity, publicly or not, ill-treatment towards a domestic or tamed animal or one held in captivity; in case of conviction of the owner of the animal or if the owner is unknown, the Court may decide the confiscation of the animal and its transfer to an animal protection organization recognized as being of public utility or declared as such, which may freely dispose of it.

12°) Failure to observe rules relating to the putting into circulation of public transport vehicles:
those who contravene the provisions of the texts on the putting into circulation of public transport vehicles, provisions relating to the weight of said vehicles; the method of their loading, the number and safety of passengers; the indication inside the vehicles of the number of seats they contain and the price of transport; the indication of the name of the owner.

13°) Damage to telephone or telegraph installations:
those who through negligence or imprudence, damage in any manner whatsoever an installation or the equipment of a telephone or telegraph installation.

Chapter 4: Third-class misdemeanours

Article 604-1: Are punished by the penalties for third-class misdemeanours

1°) Lending of firearms:
those who entrust a firearm to an inexperienced person or a person not in full possession of their faculties, without prejudice, where applicable, to the penalties provided for by the regulations on weapons and ammunition.

2°) Unauthorised shooting or fireworks:
those who, despite the prohibition by the authority, fire shots or fireworks in public places or on the public highway.

3°) Disturbance of the administration of justice:
those who disturb the administration of justice at a hearing or in any other place, without prejudice, where applicable, to the penalties imposed by law for more serious offences.

4°) Failure to observe the price of foodstuffs:
those who sell foodstuffs or food above the prices set by the authority, without prejudice to the provisions of the legislation on prices.

5°) Use of non-regulatory weights or measures:
those who use weights or measures different from those established by the laws and regulations in force.

6°) Noise disturbance:
the perpetrators or accomplices of abusive or nocturnal noise or disturbances disturbing the tranquillity of the inhabitants.

7°) Use of the telephone while driving:
those who use the telephone while driving a vehicle or handlebars of a motorised or non-motorised device.

8°) Driving vehicles without registration plates:
those who travel in vehicles or drive devices without a registration plate issued by the competent services or bearing fanciful registration plates.

9°) Extinguishing lights on the public highway:
those who extinguish lights intended to facilitate traffic on the public highway or to prevent accidents.

10°) Damage to public roads:
those who damage or deteriorate in any way whatsoever public roads or encroach upon their width;
those who, without authorisation, remove from public roads and their surroundings, plants, earth, stones, sand or materials belonging to the State.

11°) Exhibition of symbols with criminal connotations:
those who display or exhibit, except for the purposes of a film or a show or an exhibition involving a historical evocation, uniforms, insignia or emblems recalling the insignia, uniforms or emblems that have been worn by members of an organisation declared criminal or by a person found guilty by a national or international court of a crime against humanity provided for by this Code; the judgment orders, in addition to the fine, the confiscation of the aforementioned uniforms, insignia or emblems.

12°) Illegal inscriptions on property:
those who, without authorisation from the administration, make by any means whatsoever inscriptions, trace signs or drawings on movable or immovable property of the domain of the State, territorial authorities or on property located on this domain, except in order to allow the execution of a public service;
those who, without being owners, usufructuaries or tenants of an immovable property or without being authorised by one of these persons, make thereon, by any means whatsoever, inscriptions, trace signs or drawings.

13°) Diversion of water:
those who voluntarily divert or unduly use water intended for irrigation by law or by regulatory provisions emanating from the administration or distribution organisations.

14°) Fishing and use of water contrary to local customs:
those who contravene local customs relating to fishing and the use of water.

15°) Display of indecent images:
those who display or cause to be displayed on the public highway or in public places posters, images, drawings or photographs contrary to decency; in this case, in addition to the fine, the judgment of conviction orders the confiscation of the images, drawings or photographs.

16°) Abandonment of vehicle wrecks, refuse and waste:
those who abandon vehicle wrecks, refuse, waste materials and other objects transported in a vehicle.

17°) Violation of military space:
those who carry out surveys or recordings made without authorisation in a prohibited zone established by the military authority; in this case, the judgment orders the confiscation of the surveys and recordings made.

18°) Serious failure or negligence in the supervision of minor children:
parents, guardians or custodians against whom a serious failure or negligence has been established in the supervision of minor children in their care.

19°) Concealment of the face during a demonstration on the public highway:
those who, within or in the immediate vicinity of a demonstration on the public highway, voluntarily conceal their face in order not to be identified in circumstances giving rise to fears of breaches of public order; these provisions are not applicable to demonstrations in accordance with local customs or when the concealment of the face is justified by a legitimate reason.

20°) Refusal to supply goods or provide services:
those who, being a professional, refuse to supply a good or provide a service despite a request to that effect made by a consumer or another professional.

Article 604-2

Are confiscated the fireworks, weapons, weights and measures, in the cases provided for in points 1°, 2° and 5° of the preceding article.

BOOK VII

TRANSITIONAL PROVISIONS AND FINAL PROVISIONS

Chapter 1: Transitional provisions

Article 701-1

In all matters that have not been regulated by this Code and that are governed by specific laws and regulations, the courts and tribunals shall continue to observe them.

Chapter 2: Final provisions

Article702-1

Are and remain repealed all previous provisions contrary to those of this Code, in particular Law No. 01-079 of 20 August 2001, as amended, establishing the Penal Code.

Bamako, 13 December 2024

The President of the Transition, Head of State,
General Assimi GOITA

Attacks on the Physical and Psychological Integrity of the Person	321-1 to 321-70
